 107  class Labels(unittest.TestCase):
 108      def test_valid(self):
 109          self.assertEqual(b4.block_problems(*record('P', [DECISION])), [])
 110          self.assertEqual(b4.block_problems(*record('I')), [])

 112      def test_agent_cores_errors(self):
 113          self.assertTrue(b4.block_problems(*record('P', [DECISION], agents_after='123 claude')))
 114          self.assertTrue(b4.block_problems(*record('P', [DECISION], cores_before=False)))
 115          self.assertTrue(b4.block_problems(*record('P', [{**DECISION, 'cores_ok': False}])))
 116          self.assertTrue(b4.block_problems(*record('P', [DECISION, {**DECISION, 'error': 'x'}])))
 117          self.assertTrue(b4.block_problems(*record('P', [DECISION], errors=['RuntimeError: x'])))
 118          self.assertTrue(b4.block_problems(*record('P')))  # no decisions
 119  
 120      def test_violation_between_clean_endpoints(self):
 121          rec = record('I', watch=watch(['agent pid 42: node /usr/bin/claude']))
 122          self.assertEqual(rec[0]['agents_before'] + rec[0]['agents_after'], '')
 123          self.assertTrue(any('agent pid 42' in w for w in b4.block_problems(*rec)))
 124          rec = record('P', [DECISION], watch=watch(['llama-server Cpus_allowed_list:\t4-5']))
 125          self.assertTrue(any('llama-server' in w for w in b4.block_problems(*rec)))
 126          rec = record('Y', [CYCLE], watch=watch(["detector threads {7: [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5]}"]))
 127          self.assertTrue(any('detector threads' in w for w in b4.block_problems(*rec)))
 128  
 129      def test_overrun_limit(self):
 130          self.assertEqual(b4.block_problems(*record('P', [DECISION], phases=[{'phase': 'P', 'overrun_s': 15.0}])), [])
 131          self.assertTrue(b4.block_problems(*record('P', [DECISION], phases=[{'phase': 'P', 'overrun_s': 15.1}])))
 132  
 133      def test_readings(self):
 134          rec, rows = record('P', [DECISION])
 135          rec['end'] = {**rec['end'], 'skin': None}
 136          self.assertTrue(b4.block_problems(rec, rows))
 137          rec, rows = record('P', [DECISION])
 138          del rec['end']['mem_llama_server']
 139          self.assertTrue(b4.block_problems(rec, rows))
 140          rec, rows = record('Y', [CYCLE])
 141          self.assertTrue(any('robotcam' in w for w in b4.block_problems(rec, rows)))  # RobotCam memory needed
 142          rec['start']['mem_robotcam'] = rec['end']['mem_robotcam'] = {'Pss': 5, 'VmHWM': 6, 'pids': [9], 'rc': 0}
 143          self.assertEqual(b4.block_problems(rec, rows), [])
 144          rec['camera_stop'] = {'error': 'capture not shown stopped'}
 145          self.assertTrue(b4.block_problems(rec, rows))

 147      def test_partial_reads(self):
 148          base = good(mem=('runner', 'llama_server'))
 149          need = ['runner', 'llama_server']
 150          self.assertEqual(b4.reading_problems(base, 's', need), [])
 151          cases = {'battery_rc': 1, 'dump_rc': None, 'voltage_uv': None, 'charge_uah': None, 'status': None}
 152          for k, v in cases.items():
 153              self.assertTrue(b4.reading_problems({**base, k: v}, 's', need), k)
 154          self.assertTrue(b4.reading_problems({**base, 'caps': {'policy0': 'error: x'}}, 's', need))
 155          no_hwm = {**base, 'mem_llama_server': {'Pss': 1, 'pids': [1], 'rc': 1}}  # Pss read, status read failed
 156          why = b4.reading_problems(no_hwm, 's', need)
 157          self.assertTrue(any('VmHWM' in w for w in why) and any('rc 1' in w for w in why))

 174  class Comparability(unittest.TestCase):
 175      def pair(self, skin=30.5, battery=85, label='VALID'):
 176          p = {'block': 'P', 'label': 'VALID', 'start': good(30.0, 90)}
 177          pt = {'block': 'PT', 'label': label, 'start': good(skin, battery)}
 178          return b4.comparable(p, pt)
 179  
 180      def test_rule(self):
 181          self.assertEqual(self.pair()[0], 'COMPARABLE')
 182          self.assertEqual(self.pair(skin=31.0, battery=80)[0], 'COMPARABLE')  # limits inclusive
 183          self.assertEqual(self.pair(skin=31.1)[0], 'NOT COMPARABLE')
 184          self.assertEqual(self.pair(battery=79)[0], 'NOT COMPARABLE')
 185          self.assertEqual(self.pair(label='INVALID')[0], 'NOT COMPARABLE')
 186

 188  class Threads(unittest.TestCase):
 189      def test_sampler_samples_and_problems(self):
 190          t = time.monotonic()
 191          s = b4.Sampler(read=lambda: good(mono=time.monotonic()), period=0.05).start()
 192          time.sleep(0.3)
 193          res = s.stop()
 194          self.assertFalse(res['alive'])
 195          self.assertGreaterEqual(len(res['samples']), 4)
 196          self.assertEqual((res['sched']['cpus'], res['sched']['nice']), ([0, 1, 2, 3], 10))
 197          self.assertEqual(b4.sampler_problems(res, t, time.monotonic()), [])
 198          gap = {**res, 'samples': [good(mono=t + 1), good(mono=t + 20)]}
 199          self.assertTrue(any('gap' in w for w in b4.sampler_problems(gap, t, t + 21)))
 200          bad = {**res, 'samples': res['samples'] + [{**good(mono=t), 'root_rc': 1}]}
 201          self.assertTrue(any('failed' in w for w in b4.sampler_problems(bad, t, time.monotonic())))
 202          self.assertTrue(b4.sampler_problems({**res, 'error': 'OSError: x'}, t, time.monotonic()))
 203  
 204      def test_pt_sampler_needs_cores_0_3_nice_10(self):
 205          res = {'period_s': 5, 'error': None, 'alive': False, 'samples': [good(mono=1), good(mono=6)]}
 206          self.assertEqual(b4.sampler_problems({**res, 'sched': SCHED}, 0, 7), [])
 207          for sched in ({**SCHED, 'cpus': [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5]}, {**SCHED, 'nice': 0}, None):
 208              self.assertTrue(any('need' in w for w in b4.sampler_problems({**res, 'sched': sched}, 0, 7)))
 209  
 210      def test_slow_read_skips_slots(self):
 211          s = b4.Sampler(read=lambda: (time.sleep(0.12), good(mono=time.monotonic()))[1], period=0.05).start()
 212          time.sleep(0.4)
 213          res = s.stop()
 214          monos = [x['mono'] for x in res['samples']]
 215          self.assertTrue(all(b - a >= 0.1 for a, b in zip(monos, monos[1:])))  # no catch-up burst
 216  
 217      def test_watch_scans_at_start_between_and_at_stop(self):
 218          hits = iter([[], ['agent pid 7: codex'], []] + [[]] * 100)
 219          w = b4.Periodic(lambda: {'mono': time.monotonic(), 'hits': next(hits)}, 0.05).start()
 220          time.sleep(0.12)
 221          res = w.stop()
 222          self.assertGreaterEqual(len(res['samples']), 3)  # at once, every period, once more at stop
 223          self.assertTrue(any('codex' in p for p in b4.watch_problems(res)))
 224          self.assertEqual(b4.watch_problems({**watch(), 'sched': SCHED}), [])
 225  
 226      def test_watch_check_affinities(self):
 227          class Det:
 228              def __init__(self, cpus):
 229                  self.cpus = cpus
 230  
 231              def affinity(self):
 232                  return {11: self.cpus, 12: [4, 5]}
 233          saved = b4.cores_ok, b4.AGENT_RE
 234          b4.cores_ok, b4.AGENT_RE = (lambda: True), b4.re.compile('no-such-agent-name-xyz')
 235          try:
 236              self.assertEqual(b4.watch_check({'detector': Det([4, 5])})['hits'], [])
 237              self.assertTrue(b4.watch_check({'detector': Det([4, 5, 6])})['hits'])
 238              self.assertTrue(b4.watch_check({'server_pid': 10 ** 9})['hits'])  # unreadable -> a hit
 239          finally:
 240              b4.cores_ok, b4.AGENT_RE = saved
 241          self.assertEqual(b4.parse_cpus('4-7'), {4, 5, 6, 7})
 242          self.assertEqual(b4.parse_cpus('0-1,4,6-7'), {0, 1, 4, 6, 7})
 243  
 244  
 245  class Deadlines(Clocked):
 246      def picker(self, durations, dur=180.0, restore_s=0.0):
 247          it = iter(durations)
 248          acts, rows = [], []
 249  
 250          def slot(action):
 251              self.c.sleep(restore_s)
 252              acts.append(action)
 253              return {'action': action, 'wall_s': restore_s}
 254  
 255          def decide(s, item, p):
 256              d = next(it)
 257              self.c.sleep(d)
 258              return {'id': item['id'], 'wall_s': d}
 259          saved = b4.slot, b4.decide
 260          b4.slot, b4.decide = slot, decide
 261          try:
 262              s = {'items': [{'id': i} for i in b4.ITEMS]}
 263              rec = {'work_s': dur}
 264              b4.phase(rec, 'P', lambda d: b4.run_picker((s, {('V3', 'FWD', i): None for i in b4.ITEMS}, []), d,
 265                                                         rows.append))
 266          finally:
 267              b4.slot, b4.decide = saved
 268          return rows, rec['phases'][0]['overrun_s']
 269  
 270      def test_variable_durations(self):
 271          rows, over = self.picker([7, 3, 11, 5] * 30)
 272          self.assertEqual(sum(r['wall_s'] for r in rows[:-1]) < 180, True)  # the last one started before the deadline
 273          self.assertEqual(round(sum(r['wall_s'] for r in rows) - 180, 3), over)
 274          self.assertLessEqual(over, 11)
 275          self.assertEqual([r['n'] for r in rows if 'restore' in r], list(range(5, len(rows) + 1, 5)))
 276          self.assertEqual([r['id'] for r in rows[:21]], list(b4.ITEMS) + [1])
 277  
 278      def test_oversized_first_call(self):
 279          rows, over = self.picker([200])
 280          self.assertEqual((len(rows), over), (1, 20.0))
 281          self.assertTrue(b4.block_problems(*record('P', rows, phases=[{'phase': 'P', 'overrun_s': over}])))
 282  
 283      def test_restore_overhead_counts(self):
 284          rows, over = self.picker([9.0] * 50, restore_s=4.0)
 285          spent = sum(r['wall_s'] + r.get('restore', {}).get('wall_s', 0) for r in rows)
 286          self.assertEqual(round(spent - 180, 3), over)
 287          self.assertLess(spent - (rows[-1]['wall_s'] + rows[-1].get('restore', {}).get('wall_s', 0)), 180)
 288  
 289      def test_idle_and_vision(self):
 290          rec = {'work_s': 180.0}
 291          b4.phase(rec, 'I', lambda d: b4.pause(max(0.0, d - b4.clock())))
 292          self.assertEqual(rec['phases'][0]['overrun_s'], 0.0)
 293          saved = b4.post
 294          b4.post = lambda path, body: (self.c.sleep(25), {'choices': [{'message': {'content': 'x'}}], 'timings': {}})[1]
 295          try:
 296              rows, rec = [], {'work_s': 180.0}
 297              fr = {i: ({'frame_file': 'f.jpg'}, 'AAAA') for i in b4.FRAME_IDS}
 298              b4.phase(rec, 'V70', lambda d: b4.run_vision(fr, 'V70', d, rows.append))
 299          finally:
 300              b4.post = saved
 301          self.assertEqual((len(rows), rec['phases'][0]['overrun_s']), (8, 20.0))  # 8th starts at 175 s
 302  
 303  
 304  class FakeRobot:
 305      def __init__(self, frames, detect_s=0.2, fail_start=False):
 306          self.frames, self.detect_s, self.fail_start = iter(frames), detect_s, fail_start
 307          self.camera_session, self.capturing, self.stops = None, False, 0
 308          self.detector = self
 309          self.size_policy = self
 310  
 311      def start_camera(self):
 312          self.capturing = True
 313          if self.fail_start:
 314              raise RuntimeError('RobotCam did not publish a usable frame')
 315  
 316      def stop_camera(self):
 317          self.stops += 1
 318          self.capturing = False
 319  
 320      def next_size(self):
 321          return 320
 322  
 323      def observe(self, dets, h):
 324          pass
 325  
 326      def detect(self, image, size):
 327          b4.clock.sleep(self.detect_s)
 328          return []
 329  
 330      def affinity(self):
 331          return {1: [4, 5]}
 332  
 333      def config(self):
 334          return {}
 335

 337  class Image:
 338      height = 480
 339  
 340  
 341  class Camera(Clocked):
 342      def setUp(self):
 343          super().setUp()
 344          self.saved2 = b4.read_frame, b4.move_s
 345          b4.move_s = lambda: 1.5
 346  
 347      def tearDown(self):
 348          b4.read_frame, b4.move_s = self.saved2
 349          super().tearDown()
 350  
 351      def run_frames(self, frames, detect_s=0.2, dur=180.0):
 352          robot, rows, rec = FakeRobot([]), [], {'work_s': dur}
 353          it = iter(frames)
 354          b4.read_frame = lambda **kw: next(it)
 355          robot.detect_s = detect_s
 356          b4.phase(rec, 'Y', lambda d: b4.run_camera(robot, d, rows.append))
 357          return rows, rec['phases'][0]['overrun_s']
 358  
 359      def ok(self, n):
 360          return {'status': 'ok', 'session': 's', 'frame': n, 'age_s': 0.5, 'image': Image()}
 361  
 362      def test_good_cycle_then_persistent_failure_is_invalid(self):
 363          rows, _ = self.run_frames([self.ok(1)] + [{'status': 'missing'}] * 1000)
 364          self.assertEqual([r['kind'] for r in rows], ['cycle', 'camera_stop'])  # the phase ends at the first one
 365          rec, _ = record('Y', rows)
 366          rec['start']['mem_robotcam'] = rec['end']['mem_robotcam'] = {'Pss': 5, 'VmHWM': 6, 'rc': 0}
 367          self.assertTrue(any('camera stop: missing' in w for w in b4.block_problems(rec, rows)))
 368  
 369      def test_no_new_allowed_three_times(self):
 370          frames = [self.ok(1), self.ok(1), self.ok(1), self.ok(1)] + [self.ok(n) for n in range(2, 500)]
 371          rows, _ = self.run_frames(frames)
 372          self.assertEqual(sum(r['kind'] == 'camera_stop' for r in rows), 3)
 373          rec, _ = record('Y', rows)
 374          rec['start']['mem_robotcam'] = rec['end']['mem_robotcam'] = {'Pss': 5, 'VmHWM': 6, 'rc': 0}
 375          self.assertEqual(b4.block_problems(rec, rows), [])
 376          rows, _ = self.run_frames([self.ok(1)] * 10 + [self.ok(n) for n in range(2, 500)])
 377          self.assertEqual(sum(r['kind'] == 'camera_stop' for r in rows), 4)  # the 4th ends the phase
 378          self.assertTrue(any('"no new"' in w for w in b4.block_problems(record('Y', rows)[0], rows)))
 379  
 380      def test_camera_overrun_and_last_move_cut(self):
 381          rows, over = self.run_frames([self.ok(n) for n in range(1, 500)], detect_s=0.5)
 382          self.assertEqual(over, 0.0)  # the move after the last cycle is cut at the deadline
 383          self.assertTrue(all(r['t'] < 180 for r in rows))
 384          self.assertEqual(round(rows[1]['t'] - rows[0]['t'], 3), 2.0)
 385          rows, over = self.run_frames([self.ok(n) for n in range(1, 500)], detect_s=40.0)
 386          self.assertGreater(over, 0)
 387          self.assertLessEqual(over, 40.0)
 388

 390  class Ownership(unittest.TestCase):
 391      """A camera start that begins capture and then raises: stop_camera is still called; every cleanup runs."""
 392  
 393      def setUp(self):
 394          self.saved = (b4.make_robot, b4.reading, b4.cores_ok, b4.repro.agents, b4.read_frame, b4.watch_check,
 395                        b4.start_server, b4.stop_server, b4.picker_setup, b4.warm, b4.stop_camera)
 396          b4.reading = lambda pids=None, **kw: good()
 397          b4.cores_ok = lambda: True
 398          b4.repro.agents = lambda: ''
 399          b4.read_frame = lambda **kw: {'status': 'missing'}
 400          b4.watch_check = lambda targets: {'mono': 0, 'hits': []}
 401  
 402      def tearDown(self):
 403          (b4.make_robot, b4.reading, b4.cores_ok, b4.repro.agents, b4.read_frame, b4.watch_check,
 404           b4.start_server, b4.stop_server, b4.picker_setup, b4.warm, b4.stop_camera) = self.saved
 405  
 406      def ctx(self, smoke=False):
 407          return {'session': 1, 'smoke': smoke, 'dur': 0.05, 'test': {}, 'frames': {}}
 408  
 409      def test_failed_start_is_stopped(self):
 410          robot = FakeRobot([], fail_start=True)
 411          b4.make_robot = lambda: robot
 412          rec, rows = b4.run_block('Y', Path('.'), self.ctx(), lambda r: None)
 413          self.assertEqual(robot.stops, 1)
 414          self.assertFalse(robot.capturing)
 415          self.assertEqual(rec['label'], 'INVALID')
 416  
 417      def test_each_cleanup_runs_when_another_fails(self):
 418          robot = FakeRobot([], fail_start=True)
 419          b4.make_robot = lambda: robot
 420          stopped = []
 421  
 422          class Proc:
 423              pid = 4242
 424          b4.start_server = lambda out, name, cmd: (Proc(), None, 0.1)
 425          b4.stop_server = lambda proc, log: stopped.append(proc) or (0, False)
 426          b4.picker_setup = lambda test: ({'items': [{'id': 1}]}, {('V3', 'FWD', 1): None}, [])
 427          b4.warm = lambda prefix: {}
 428          b4.stop_camera = lambda r: (_ for _ in ()).throw(OSError('broadcast failed'))
 429          with unittest.mock.patch.object(b4.Periodic, 'stop', side_effect=RuntimeError('join')), \
 430                  unittest.mock.patch.object(b4, 'decide', return_value={'id': 1, 'wall_s': 0.0}), \
 431                  unittest.mock.patch.object(b4, 'slot', return_value={'action': 'restore', 'wall_s': 0.0}):
 432              rec, rows = b4.run_block('PY', Path('.'), {**self.ctx(), 'dur': 0.01}, lambda r: None)
 433          self.assertEqual(len(stopped), 1)  # the server is stopped although watch and camera cleanup failed
 434          self.assertTrue(any('cleanup camera' in e for e in rec['errors']))
 435          self.assertTrue(any('cleanup watch' in e for e in rec['errors']))
 436          self.assertEqual(rec['label'], 'INVALID')
 437  
 438      def test_initial_checks_abort_before_any_setup(self):
 439          b4.repro.agents = lambda: '42 claude'
 440          b4.start_server = lambda *a: self.fail('server started')
 441          b4.make_robot = lambda: self.fail('camera started')
 442          rec, rows = b4.run_block('PY', Path('.'), self.ctx(), lambda r: None)
 443          self.assertEqual(rec['label'], 'INVALID')
 444          self.assertIn('no workload started', ' '.join(rec['errors']))
 445

 447  class Definition(unittest.TestCase):
 448      def test_changes_are_refused(self):
 449          d = {'files': {'main.py': 'a', 'benchmark/x.py': 'b'}, 'commands': {'P': 'x'}, 'frames': {'1': 'f'}}
 450          self.assertEqual(b4.definition_diff(d, json.loads(json.dumps(d))), [])
 451          self.assertEqual(b4.definition_diff(d, {**d, 'files': {**d['files'], 'main.py': 'z'}}),
 452                           [('files', 'main.py', 'a', 'z')])
 453          self.assertTrue(b4.definition_diff(d, {**d, 'frames': {'1': 'g'}}))
 454          self.assertTrue(b4.definition_diff(d, {**d, 'files': {**d['files'], 'server_manager.py': 'c'}}))  # new dep
 455          self.assertTrue(b4.definition_diff(d, {**d, 'commands': {'P': 'x --threads 8'}}))
 456  
 457      def test_real_definition(self):
 458          fr = b4.frames()
 459          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as t:
 460              d = b4.definition(Path(t), fr)
 461          files = d['files']
 462          for f in ('benchmark/mind_picker/b4_baseline/b4.py', 'benchmark/mind_picker/b4_baseline/oneshot.sh',
 463                    'main.py', 'server_manager.py', 'detect_person.py', 'benchmark/mind_picker/bc_build/bc.py'):
 464              self.assertIn(f, files)
 465          self.assertEqual(sorted(d['frames']), sorted(str(i) for i in b4.FRAME_IDS))
 466          self.assertEqual(set(d['commands']), set(b4.SERVER_BLOCKS))
 467  
 468      def test_resume_refuses_a_changed_frame(self):
 469          fr = b4.frames()
 470          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as t:
 471              before = b4.definition(Path(t), fr)
 472              frame = b4.bv.FRAMES_DIR / Path(fr[1][0]['frame_file']).name
 473              with unittest.mock.patch.object(b4, 'sha', side_effect=lambda b: 'changed' if b == frame.read_bytes()
 474                                              else b4.bc.sha(b)):
 475                  after = b4.definition(Path(t), fr)
 476          self.assertEqual([x[:2] for x in b4.definition_diff(before, after)], [('frames', '1')])
 477

 479  class Commands(unittest.TestCase):
 480      def test_v70_v140(self):
 481          out = Path('/run/x')
 482          base = b4.bc.command(out / 'slots')
 483          for k in ('P', 'P0', 'PT', 'PY', 'V70'):
 484              self.assertEqual(b4.server_command(out, k), base)
 485          v140 = b4.server_command(out, 'V140')
 486          diff = [(a, b) for a, b in zip(base, v140) if a != b]
 487          self.assertEqual(diff, [('70', '140'), ('70', '140')])
 488          self.assertEqual(v140[v140.index('--image-min-tokens') + 1], '140')
 489          self.assertEqual(b4.ORDER, ('I', 'P', 'P0', 'PT', 'V70', 'V140', 'Y', 'PY'))

 492  class Sessions(unittest.TestCase):
 493      def setUp(self):
 494          self.saved = b4.gate, b4.run_block, b4.reading, b4.cores_ok, b4.repro.agents, b4.cr.wait_cores
 495          self.ran = []
 496          b4.reading = lambda **kw: good()
 497          b4.cores_ok = lambda: True
 498          b4.repro.agents = lambda: ''
 499          b4.cr.wait_cores = lambda label: None
 500          b4.gate = lambda ref, label: {'passed': True}
 501  
 502      def tearDown(self):
 503          b4.gate, b4.run_block, b4.reading, b4.cores_ok, b4.repro.agents, b4.cr.wait_cores = self.saved
 504  
 505      def fake_blocks(self, labels):
 506          def run_block(k, out, ctx, emit):
 507              self.ran.append(k)
 508              return {'block': k, 'label': labels.get(k, 'VALID'), 'problems': []}, []
 509          b4.run_block = run_block
 510  
 511      def session(self, out, smoke=False):
 512          ctx = {'smoke': smoke, 'session': 1, 'dur': 1}
 513          with open(out / 'blocks.jsonl', 'a') as blog, unittest.mock.patch('sys.stdout', io.StringIO()):
 514              return b4.session_loop(out, {'start': {'skin': 30.0}}, ctx, lambda r: None, blog)
 515  
 516      def test_order_invalid_stops_and_resume_redoes_it(self):
 517          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 518              out = Path(d)
 519              self.fake_blocks({'PT': 'INVALID'})
 520              self.assertIn('block PT INVALID', self.session(out))
 521              self.assertEqual(self.ran, ['I', 'P', 'P0', 'PT'])
 522              self.ran.clear()
 523              self.fake_blocks({})
 524              self.assertEqual(self.session(out), 'all blocks done')
 525              self.assertEqual(self.ran, ['PT', 'V70', 'V140', 'Y', 'PY'])
 526              self.assertEqual(b4.next_blocks(b4.read_jsonl(out / 'blocks.jsonl')), [])
 527  
 528      def test_gate_agent_and_safety_defer_before_the_block(self):
 529          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 530              out = Path(d)
 531              self.fake_blocks({})
 532              b4.gate = lambda ref, label: {'passed': False, 'skin': 33.0, 'waited_s': 480}
 533              self.assertIn('deferred before block I', self.session(out))
 534              b4.gate = lambda ref, label: {'passed': True}
 535              b4.repro.agents = lambda: '42 claude'
 536              self.assertIn('agent processes running', self.session(out))
 537              b4.repro.agents = lambda: ''
 538              b4.reading = lambda **kw: {**good(), 'status': b4.STATUS_STOP}
 539              self.assertIn('stopped before block I', self.session(out))
 540              self.assertEqual(self.ran, [])
 541              self.assertEqual(b4.read_jsonl(out / 'blocks.jsonl'), [])
 542  
 543      def test_smoke_runs_every_block_once_gating_only_the_first(self):
 544          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 545              self.fake_blocks({k: b4.SMOKE_LABEL for k in b4.ORDER})
 546              gated = []
 547              b4.gate = lambda ref, label: gated.append(label) or {'passed': True}
 548              b4.repro.agents = lambda: '42 claude'
 549              self.assertEqual(self.session(Path(d), smoke=True), 'all blocks done')
 550              self.assertEqual(self.ran, list(b4.ORDER))
 551              self.assertEqual(gated, [f'block {b4.ORDER[0]}'])
 552  
 553      def test_timed_session_gates_every_block(self):
 554          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 555              self.fake_blocks({})
 556              gated = []
 557              b4.gate = lambda ref, label: gated.append(label) or {'passed': True}
 558              self.assertEqual(self.session(Path(d)), 'all blocks done')
 559              self.assertEqual(gated, [f'block {k}' for k in b4.ORDER])
 560  
 561      def test_signal_in_a_block_keeps_the_record_and_stops(self):
 562          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 563              out = Path(d)
 564              b4.run_block = lambda k, o, c, e: ({'block': k, 'label': 'INVALID', 'problems': ['x'],
 565                                                  'interrupted': True}, [])
 566              with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
 567                  self.session(out)
 568              self.assertEqual(len(b4.read_jsonl(out / 'blocks.jsonl')), 1)
 569

 571  class Readings(unittest.TestCase):
 572      OUT = ('=== battery\n88\nDischarging\n301\n2900000\n4100000\nrc=0\n'
 573             '=== thermalservice\nThermal Status: 1\nCurrent temperatures from HAL:\n'
 574             '\tTemperature{mValue=33.5, mType=3, mName=VIRTUAL-SKIN, mStatus=0}\nrc=0\n'
 575             '=== mem_llama_server\npid: 7\nRss: 300 kB\nPss: 200 kB\nVmHWM: 400 kB\nVmRSS: 300 kB\nrc=0\n'
 576             '=== mem_robotcam\npid: 8\nPss: 10 kB\npid: 9\nPss: 5 kB\nVmHWM: 20 kB\nrc=0\n')
 577  
 578      def read(self, out):
 579          saved = cr.root
 580          cr.root = lambda script, tag, timeout: (0, out)
 581          try:
 582              return b4.reading({'llama_server': 7})
 583          finally:
 584              cr.root = saved
 585  
 586      def test_parse_root_output(self):
 587          c = self.read(self.OUT)
 588          self.assertEqual((c['battery_pct'], c['charge_uah'], c['status'], c['skin']), (88, 2900000, 1, 33.5))
 589          self.assertEqual((c['battery_rc'], c['dump_rc']), (0, 0))
 590          self.assertEqual(c['mem_llama_server']['Pss'], 200)
 591          self.assertEqual(c['mem_robotcam'], {'pids': [8, 9], 'Pss': 15, 'VmHWM': 20, 'rc': 0})
 592          self.assertEqual(b4.reading_problems(c, 's', ['llama_server', 'robotcam']), [])
 593  
 594      def test_unfinished_and_failed_sections(self):
 595          cut = self.OUT.split('=== mem_robotcam')[0].replace('VmHWM: 400 kB\nVmRSS: 300 kB\nrc=0', 'rc=1')
 596          c = self.read(cut)
 597          why = b4.reading_problems(c, 's', ['llama_server', 'robotcam'])
 598          self.assertTrue(any('llama_server section rc 1' in w for w in why))
 599          self.assertTrue(any('robotcam section rc None' in w for w in why))
 600          c = self.read(self.OUT.replace('Thermal Status: 1\n', '').replace('4100000\nrc=0', 'rc=1'))
 601          why = b4.reading_problems(c, 's')
 602          self.assertTrue(any('battery_rc 1' in w for w in why) and any('status missing' in w for w in why))
 603  
 604      def test_root_scripts_parse_in_cr_root_wrapper(self):
 605          for light in (True, False):  # the native smoke failed on '; ;' (light script, trailing ';')
 606              script = b4.root_script({'runner': 1, 'llama_server': 2}, light)
 607              wrapped = f'{{ {script}; }} </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1; rc=$?; exit $rc'  # coresidency.root's form
 608              r = b4.subprocess.run(['sh', '-n', '-c', wrapped], capture_output=True, text=True)
 609              self.assertEqual((r.returncode, r.stderr), (0, ''), light)
 610  
 611      def test_incomplete_launch_start_refuses_cleanly(self):
 612          saved = b4.reading
 613          try:
 614              for bad in ({'skin': None}, {'status': None}, {'voltage_uv': None}, {'root_rc': 1, 'root_stderr': 'x'}):
 615                  b4.reading = lambda **kw: {**good(), **bad}
 616                  with self.assertRaises(SystemExit) as e:
 617                      b4.launch_start()
 618                  self.assertIn('incomplete launch-start reading', str(e.exception))
 619              b4.reading = lambda **kw: good(31.0)
 620              self.assertEqual(b4.launch_start()['skin'], 31.0)
 621          finally:
 622              b4.reading = saved
 623  
 624      def test_heat_and_energy(self):
 625          a, b = good(30.0, mono=0), {**good(33.0, mono=180), 'charge_uah': 2990000}
 626          self.assertEqual(b4.heat(a, b), (3.0, 3.0, 1.0))
 627          self.assertEqual(b4.energy(a, b), (10.0, 144.0))
 628  
 629      def test_report(self):
 630          p = {**record('P')[0], 'label': 'VALID', 'problems': [], 'session': 1,
 631               'start': {**good(30.0), 'avail_kb': 3000000}}
 632          pt = {**record('PT')[0], 'label': 'VALID', 'problems': [], 'session': 2,
 633                'warm': {'prefix_tokens': 731, 'prompt_n': 731, 'save': {'wall_s': 0.01}}}
 634          py = {**record('PY')[0], 'label': 'INVALID', 'problems': ['x'], 'session': 2, 'handoff': good(mono=90),
 635                'camera': {'start_s': 2.0, 'affinity_start': [4, 5]}, 'camera_stop': {'stopped_s': 1.0}}
 636          dec = {'kind': 'decision', 'wall_s': 6.0, 'reason_tokens': 13, 'gen_tok_s': 6.2, 'step1_prompt_n': 85,
 637                 'step1_cache_n': 731, 'step2_prompt_n': 3, 'step2_cache_n': 828, 'endpoint_s': {'/tokenize': 0.06}}
 638          rows = [{**dec, 'block': 'P', 'session': 1}, {**dec, 'block': 'PT', 'session': 2, 'wall_s': 6.6},
 639                  {**dec, 'block': 'PY', 'session': 2},
 640                  {'kind': 'cycle', 'block': 'PY', 'session': 2, 't': 0.0, 'age_s': 0.4, 'size': 320, 'detect_ms': 150},
 641                  {'kind': 'cycle', 'block': 'PY', 'session': 2, 't': 1.8, 'age_s': 0.6, 'size': 640, 'detect_ms': 520}]
 642          run = {'utc': 'T', 'git_head': 'h', 'start': good(), 'smoke': False, 'work_s': 180,
 643                 'definition': {'files': {'benchmark/mind_picker/b4_baseline/b4.py': 'c' * 64},
 644                                'commands': {'P': 'x', 'V140': 'y'}, 'frames': {}}}
 645          text = b4.report(run, [p, pt, py], rows)
 646          self.assertIn('P vs PT (monitoring cost): COMPARABLE', text)
 647          self.assertIn('PT/P 1.100', text)
 648          self.assertIn('== PY: INVALID', text)
 649          self.assertIn(' Y phase start', text)
 650          self.assertIn('== V70: not run', text)
 651          self.assertIn('== V140: not run', text)
 652          self.assertIn('code cccccccccccc', text)
 653  
 654  
 655  if __name__ == '__main__':
 656      unittest.main()
END
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b4_baseline/oneshot.sh (143 lines)
   1  #!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash
   2  # MIND-B4: timed baselines (picker, vision call, RobotCam+YOLO, idle, monitoring cost); no motors, serial port or
   3  # mission. Start from NATIVE Termux (prompt "~ $"), no agent running, charger unplugged, battery >= 80 %, Termux in
   4  # front, screen on:
   5  #   bash ~/robot/benchmark/mind_picker/b4_baseline/oneshot.sh                  (session 1: a new run)
   6  #   bash ~/robot/benchmark/mind_picker/b4_baseline/oneshot.sh --resume <UTC>   (next session: remaining blocks)
   7  #   bash ~/robot/benchmark/mind_picker/b4_baseline/oneshot.sh --smoke          (20 s blocks, NOT A MEASUREMENT;
   8  #                                                                               an agent may stay resident)
   9  # MEM-1's oneshot.sh (memory_context/) with this folder's runner: same root, battery, lock, screen-timeout and signal
  10  # handling, the same 15 s root thermal logger (pinned to cores 0-3 at nice 10; the skin gate reads its log).
  11  # Root commands that call Android services use the DECISIONS #123 form:
  12  # su -c "<cmd> </dev/null >/data/local/tmp/<file> 2>&1", then the file is read.
  13  H=/data/data/com.termux/files/home
  14  L=$H/robot_runs/mind_b4
  15  R=$H/robot/benchmark/mind_picker/b4_baseline
  16  S=/data/local/tmp/mind_b4_screen.txt
  17  mkdir -p "$L"
  18  CONSOLE=$L/oneshot_console_$(date -u +%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ).log
  19  say() { echo "[oneshot] $*" | tee -a "$CONSOLE"; }
  20  
  21  if [ -d /termux-home ] || [ "${PREFIX:-}" != /data/data/com.termux/files/usr ]; then
  22    echo "Run this from native Termux (~ \$), not from Debian."; exit 1
  23  fi
  24  su -c id >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "No root: su failed. Check Magisk."; exit 1; }
  25  [ -f "$R/b4.py" ] || { echo "Missing $R/b4.py"; exit 1; }
  26  case "$*" in
  27    ""|"--smoke"|"--resume "[0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9]T[0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9]Z) ;;
  28    *) echo "Usage: oneshot.sh [--smoke | --resume <UTC>]"; exit 1;;
  29  esac
  30  if pgrep -fa 'claude|agy|node|codex' >/dev/null; then
  31    if [ "$*" = --smoke ]; then
  32      echo "[oneshot] agent running (allowed for --smoke only; NOT A MEASUREMENT):"; pgrep -fa 'claude|agy|node|codex'
  33    else
  34      echo "An agent is still running:"; pgrep -fa 'claude|agy|node|codex'; echo "Quit it, then rerun."; exit 1
  35    fi
  36  fi
  37  if pgrep -fa 'llama-server|chat\.py' >/dev/null; then
  38    echo "llama-server or chat.py is already running:"; pgrep -fa 'llama-server|chat\.py'; echo "Stop it, then rerun."; exit 1
  39  fi
  40  RUNNERS='b4\.py|bc\.py|bv\.py|b[0-9]b?\.py|mem1\.py|chat_mtp\.py|repro\.py|vis3d\.py|power_map\.py|coresidency\.py|thermal_char\.py|vis[0-9]_run\.py|run_mission\.py|main\.py'
  41  if pgrep -fa "$RUNNERS" >/dev/null; then
  42    echo "A benchmark runner or the robot is already running:"; pgrep -fa "$RUNNERS"; echo "Stop it, then rerun."; exit 1
  43  fi
  44  check_battery() {  # checked at start and again right before the runner
  45    BAT=$(su -c 'cat /sys/class/power_supply/battery/status' 2>/dev/null)
  46    if [ "$BAT" != Discharging ]; then
  47      echo "[oneshot] Battery status is '$BAT', not Discharging: unplug the charger (power numbers would be invalid)." | tee -a "$CONSOLE"
  48      exit 1
  49    fi
  50  }
  51  cleanup() {
  52    [ -n "$LOCKED" ] || return 0  # refused before taking the lock: another run's files are not ours to touch
  53    touch "$L/.stop_thermal"
  54    if [ "$SCREEN_SET" = 1 ] && [[ $OLD_TIMEOUT =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
  55      su -c "settings put system screen_off_timeout $OLD_TIMEOUT </dev/null >$S 2>&1"
  56      su -c "settings get system screen_off_timeout </dev/null >$S 2>&1"
  57      BACK=$(su -c "cat $S" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')
  58      if [ "$BACK" = "$OLD_TIMEOUT" ]; then
  59        say "screen timeout restored to $OLD_TIMEOUT ms; read back: $BACK"
  60      else
  61        say "WARNING: screen timeout NOT restored (read back '$BACK', expected $OLD_TIMEOUT). Run in native Termux:"
  62        say "  su -c \"settings put system screen_off_timeout $OLD_TIMEOUT </dev/null >$S 2>&1\""
  63        RESTORE_FAILED=1
  64      fi
  65    fi
  66    [ -z "${PAUSE:-}" ] || kill "$PAUSE" 2>/dev/null
  67    termux-wake-unlock
  68    # the lock goes only once the logger has exited (it polls .stop_thermal every 5 s):
  69    # otherwise a new run could remove .stop_thermal under it and share its files with it
  70    JOBS="${LOGGER:-}"
  71    for i in $(seq 40); do
  72      ALIVE=; for p in $JOBS; do kill -0 "$p" 2>/dev/null && ALIVE=1; done
  73      [ -z "$ALIVE" ] && break
  74      sleep 0.5
  75    done
  76    if [ -n "$ALIVE" ]; then
  77      say "WARNING: logger still running after 20 s; $L/.lock kept. Remove it when they have stopped."
  78      RESTORE_FAILED=1
  79    else
  80      say "stopped logger"
  81      rm -rf "$L/.lock"
  82    fi
  83    [ -z "${RESTORE_FAILED:-}" ] || exit 1
  84  }
  85  LOCKED=
  86  SCREEN_SET=0
  87  trap cleanup EXIT
  88  RUNNER=
  89  LAUNCHING=
  90  PENDING=
  91  on_signal() {  # before the runner starts: exit (the EXIT trap cleans up); after: forward it and keep waiting
  92    if [ -n "$RUNNER" ]; then kill -TERM "$RUNNER" 2>/dev/null
  93    elif [ -n "$LAUNCHING" ]; then PENDING=1  # between `&` and RUNNER=$!: forwarded right after
  94    else exit "$1"; fi
  95  }
  96  trap 'on_signal 143' TERM; trap 'on_signal 129' HUP; trap 'on_signal 130' INT
  97  # one run at a time: runs share thermal.log, .stop_thermal and port 8080
  98  if ! mkdir "$L/.lock" 2>/dev/null; then
  99    echo "Another MIND-B4 launcher holds $L/.lock (pid $(cat "$L/.lock/pid" 2>/dev/null))."
 100    echo "If no run is active (for example after a crash), remove it: rm -r $L/.lock"; exit 1
 101  fi
 102  LOCKED=1
 103  echo $$ > "$L/.lock/pid"
 104  check_battery
 105  
 106  termux-wake-lock
 107  pause() { sleep "$1" & PAUSE=$!; wait "$PAUSE"; PAUSE=; }  # a trapped signal interrupts wait, not a foreground sleep
 108  # keep the screen on (Android moves Termux off the big cores when it is not in front)
 109  OLD_TIMEOUT=
 110  if su -c "settings get system screen_off_timeout </dev/null >$S 2>&1"; then
 111    OLD_TIMEOUT=$(su -c "cat $S" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')
 112  fi
 113  if ! [[ $OLD_TIMEOUT =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
 114    say "Could not read the screen timeout (read '$OLD_TIMEOUT': $(su -c "cat $S" 2>/dev/null | head -c 200)); not started."; exit 1
 115  fi
 116  SCREEN_SET=1  # from here on, cleanup restores the validated old value
 117  su -c "settings put system screen_off_timeout 2147483647 </dev/null >$S 2>&1"
 118  su -c "settings get system screen_off_timeout </dev/null >$S 2>&1"
 119  NOW=$(su -c "cat $S" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')
 120  if [ "$NOW" != 2147483647 ]; then
 121    say "Could not set the screen timeout (read back '$NOW'); not started."; exit 1
 122  fi
 123  say "screen timeout was $OLD_TIMEOUT ms; set to 2147483647 for the run (read back)"
 124  [ "$OLD_TIMEOUT" = 2147483647 ] && say "WARNING: the saved timeout is already 2147483647 (left by an earlier run?); set it yourself afterwards"
 125  rm -f "$L/.stop_thermal" "$L/thermal_logger_sched.txt"
 126  
 127  su -c "taskset -p f \$\$ >/dev/null 2>&1; renice -n 10 -p \$\$ >/dev/null 2>&1; { grep Cpus_allowed_list /proc/\$\$/status; echo nice=\$(cut -d' ' -f19 /proc/\$\$/stat); } > $L/thermal_logger_sched.txt 2>&1; while [ ! -f $L/.stop_thermal ]; do echo \"\$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ) z9=\$(cat /sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone9/temp) z10=\$(cat /sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone10/temp) z11=\$(cat /sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone11/temp) CPU-core_reading,_not_a_heat_state=1\" >> $L/thermal.log; sleep 15; done" &
 128  LOGGER=$!
 129  
 130  pause 6
 131  say "CPU-core reading, not a heat state: $(tail -1 "$L/thermal.log")"
 132  check_battery
 133  
 134  # The runner runs as a job so TERM/HUP/INT sent to this launcher reach it: it stops llama-server
 135  # itself, and we wait for that before the EXIT trap restores the screen timeout.
 136  LAUNCHING=1
 137  env PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1 python -u "$R/b4.py" "$@" > >(tee -a "$CONSOLE") 2>&1 &
 138  RUNNER=$!
 139  [ -z "$PENDING" ] || kill -TERM "$RUNNER" 2>/dev/null
 140  while kill -0 "$RUNNER" 2>/dev/null; do wait "$RUNNER"; done
 141  wait "$RUNNER"; RC=$?
 142  if [ "$RC" = 0 ]; then say "done; console log: $CONSOLE"; else say "RUNNER FAILED (exit $RC); console log: $CONSOLE"; fi
 143  exit "$RC"
END
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b4_baseline/README.md (45 lines)
   1  # MIND-B4 — timed baselines: picker, vision call, RobotCam+YOLO, idle, monitoring cost
   2  
   3  Research only. Measured alone, with no agent running: how long one picker decision takes, what one vision call
   4  costs (70 and 140 image tokens), how RobotCam+YOLO behave, how fast each heats the phone, what RAM each uses, and how
   5  much 5 s root monitoring slows the picker. Timings count only from the owner's `oneshot.sh` run in native Termux with
   6  no agent resident. No motors (`motors.py` is never imported), no serial port, no mission, no MTP.
   7  
   8  - Server (P, P0, PT, V70, V140, PY): BC's b11716 command, `bc.command(<run>/slots)` (setup_q4 flags, b11716
   9    `llama-server`, Q8_0 projector, `--image-min/max-tokens 70`, `--slot-save-path <run>/slots`); V140 changes only
  10    `--image-min-tokens` and `--image-max-tokens` to 140. Pins `bc.PINS`; one launch per block; request timeout 60 s.
  11  - Picker: B2 V3 unchanged (`b2.preflight` / `b2.score`), no memory, B2 TEST items 1–20 FWD, repeated; the common
  12    prompt prefix sent once and slot 0 saved before the block (not timed); slot restored before the 5th, 10th, …
  13    decision.
  14  - Vision: BV ids 1, 5, 6, 7, 9, 10, 16, 24, 30, 33 (frame SHA-256 checked), `bv.body`, repeated; V70 and V140 use the
  15    same frames, prompt and settings.
  16  - Camera: `main.Robot(motors=None)` is created first, then `start_camera()`; `stop_camera()` is attempted whenever a
  17    start was attempted (rate 1, PinnedDetector cpus 4–5, #136). Each cycle is `run_cycle`'s vision step plus the dry
  18    run's 1.5 s move time; no navigation, picker or heat control. "no new" frames: at most 3 per block; any other camera
  19    stop ends the phase and makes the block INVALID.
  20  - Blocks I, P, P0, PT, V70, V140, Y, PY. Each work phase has an absolute 180 s deadline: nothing new starts after it,
  21    the operation in flight may finish, the overrun is recorded and must be ≤ 15 s (P0, two cold decisions, has no
  22    deadline). PT = P + root sampler every 5 s (its thread must get cores 0–3 at nice 10); PY = a P phase, then right
  23    away a Y phase with the server idle (hand-off reading after the camera start).
  24  - Before each block: cores 4–7, agent check, MEM-1 skin gate (launch-start skin + 1.0 °C, idle wait at most
  25    30 min per block, recorded as cooling data), status < 3,
  26    battery < 45 °C; a gate not passed, an agent or lost cores ends the session before the block, and the block itself
  27    checks agent and cores again before any setup. During every block a watcher (cores 0–3, nice 10) scans `/proc`
  28    every 30 s plus at the work start and end for claude/agy/codex/node processes and the affinities (runner cores
  29    4–7, llama-server 4–7, detector threads 4–5); any hit makes the block INVALID. Resolution 30 s: an agent that lives
  30    entirely between two scans is not seen. Every reading's section return codes and required fields are checked.
  31    An INVALID block ends the session and is redone in the next. Details: docstring of `b4.py`.
  32  - Frozen definition: the first session records the SHA-256 of `oneshot.sh` and every imported module file under the
  33    repo, every server block's full command and the SHA-256 of the 10 frame files; `--resume` refuses any difference.
  34  - Labels: block VALID / INVALID; P vs PT COMPARABLE only if both VALID, start skin within 1.0 °C and battery within
  35    10 %; other cross-block numbers descriptive; energy from the charge counter INDICATIVE; server exit code ≠ 0 is a
  36    FINDING (#145).
  37  - Run (native Termux, no agent): `bash ~/robot/benchmark/mind_picker/b4_baseline/oneshot.sh`, then
  38    `… oneshot.sh --resume <UTC>` until the session ends with `all blocks done`. `--smoke`: every block once for 20 s,
  39    gate before the first block only (MEM-1's default wait; later blocks ungated), agent/cores checks recorded but not enforced, SMOKE - NOT A MEASUREMENT.
  40  - Writes `~/robot_runs/mind_b4/<UTC>/`: run.json, sessionNN.json, sessionNN_end.json, blocks.jsonl, requests.jsonl,
  41    NN_<block>_server.log, slots/, report.txt, artifacts.sha256 (the last two rewritten at each session end); the
  42    launcher's console logs and `thermal.log` are in `~/robot_runs/mind_b4/`.
  43  - Offline tests: `python -B test_b4.py` (fakes; no server, camera, root or phone).
  44  - Known limitation carried over from B0: an interrupt during resource start can leave cleanup incomplete; each
  45    cleanup action (watcher, sampler, camera, server) is protected separately.
END

+def parse_cpus(text):
+    cpus = set()
+    for part in text.strip().split(','):
+        a, _, b = part.partition('-')
+        cpus.update(range(int(a), int(b or a) + 1))
+    return cpus
+
+
+def watch_check(targets):
+    """One light /proc scan: agent processes, the runner's cores 4-7, llama-server's affinity (targets['server_pid'])
+    4-7, the detector threads' (targets['detector']) 4-5. Returns {'mono', 'hits'} (no hits: clean)."""
+    hits = []
+    for p in Path('/proc').iterdir():
+        if p.name.isdigit():
+            try:
+                cmd = (p / 'cmdline').read_bytes().replace(b'\0', b' ').decode(errors='replace')
+            except OSError:
+                continue
+            if AGENT_RE.search(cmd):
+                hits.append(f'agent pid {p.name}: {cmd[:100]}')
+    if not cores_ok():
+        hits.append(f'runner cores {sorted(cr.allowed_cpus())}')
+    if (pid := targets.get('server_pid')) is not None:
+        try:
+            line = next(l for l in Path(f'/proc/{pid}/status').read_text().splitlines()
+                        if l.startswith('Cpus_allowed_list'))
+            if parse_cpus(line.split(':', 1)[1]) != SERVER_CPUS:
+                hits.append(f'llama-server {line}')
+        except (OSError, StopIteration, ValueError) as e:
+            hits.append(f'llama-server affinity unreadable: {e}')
+    if (det := targets.get('detector')) is not None:
+        bad = {t: c for t, c in det.affinity().items() if c != DETECTOR_CPUS}
+        if bad:
+            hits.append(f'detector threads {bad}')
+    return {'mono': round(time.monotonic(), 3), 'hits': hits}
+
+
+def Watch(targets, period=WATCH_S):
+    return Periodic(lambda: watch_check(targets), period)
+
+
+def watch_problems(w):
+    why = thread_problems(w, 'watch')
+    why += [f'watch: {h}' for x in w['samples'] for h in x['hits']]
+    return why if w['samples'] else why + ['watch: no scan']

+def phase(rec, name, work):
+    """Runs work(deadline) with an absolute deadline BLOCK_S (or the smoke's) after now; records the overrun."""
+    deadline = clock() + rec['work_s']
+    work(deadline)
+    end = clock()
+    rec.setdefault('phases', []).append({'phase': name, 'overrun_s': round(max(0.0, end - deadline), 3)})

+class FakeClock:
+    def __init__(self):
+        self.t = 1000.0
+
+    def __call__(self):
+        return self.t
+
+    def sleep(self, s):
+        self.t += s
+
+
+class Clocked(unittest.TestCase):
+    """b4.clock / b4.pause replaced by a fake clock that only operations advance."""
+
+    def setUp(self):
+        self.saved = b4.clock, b4.pause, b4.cores_ok
+        self.c = FakeClock()
+        b4.clock, b4.pause, b4.cores_ok = self.c, self.c.sleep, lambda: True
+
+    def tearDown(self):
+        b4.clock, b4.pause, b4.cores_ok = self.saved

+class FakeRobot:
+    def __init__(self, frames, detect_s=0.2, fail_start=False):
+        self.frames, self.detect_s, self.fail_start = iter(frames), detect_s, fail_start
+        self.camera_session, self.capturing, self.stops = None, False, 0
+        self.detector = self
+        self.size_policy = self
+
+    def start_camera(self):
+        self.capturing = True
+        if self.fail_start:
+            raise RuntimeError('RobotCam did not publish a usable frame')
+
+    def stop_camera(self):
+        self.stops += 1
+        self.capturing = False
+
+    def next_size(self):
+        return 320
+
+    def observe(self, dets, h):
+        pass
+
+    def detect(self, image, size):
+        b4.clock.sleep(self.detect_s)
+        return []
+
+    def affinity(self):
+        return {1: [4, 5]}
+
+    def config(self):
+        return {}
+
+
+class Image:
+    height = 480
+

+class Camera(Clocked):
+    def setUp(self):
+        super().setUp()
+        self.saved2 = b4.read_frame, b4.move_s
+        b4.move_s = lambda: 1.5
+
+    def tearDown(self):
+        b4.read_frame, b4.move_s = self.saved2
+        super().tearDown()
+
+    def run_frames(self, frames, detect_s=0.2, dur=180.0):
+        robot, rows, rec = FakeRobot([]), [], {'work_s': dur}
+        it = iter(frames)
+        b4.read_frame = lambda **kw: next(it)
+        robot.detect_s = detect_s
+        b4.phase(rec, 'Y', lambda d: b4.run_camera(robot, d, rows.append))
+        return rows, rec['phases'][0]['overrun_s']
+
+    def ok(self, n):
+        return {'status': 'ok', 'session': 's', 'frame': n, 'age_s': 0.5, 'image': Image()}
+
+    def test_good_cycle_then_persistent_failure_is_invalid(self):
+        rows, _ = self.run_frames([self.ok(1)] + [{'status': 'missing'}] * 1000)
+        self.assertEqual([r['kind'] for r in rows], ['cycle', 'camera_stop'])  # the phase ends at the first one
+        rec, _ = record('Y', rows)
+        rec['start']['mem_robotcam'] = rec['end']['mem_robotcam'] = {'Pss': 5, 'VmHWM': 6, 'rc': 0}
+        self.assertTrue(any('camera stop: missing' in w for w in b4.block_problems(rec, rows)))
+
+    def test_no_new_allowed_three_times(self):
+        frames = [self.ok(1), self.ok(1), self.ok(1), self.ok(1)] + [self.ok(n) for n in range(2, 500)]
+        rows, _ = self.run_frames(frames)
+        self.assertEqual(sum(r['kind'] == 'camera_stop' for r in rows), 3)
+        rec, _ = record('Y', rows)
+        rec['start']['mem_robotcam'] = rec['end']['mem_robotcam'] = {'Pss': 5, 'VmHWM': 6, 'rc': 0}
+        self.assertEqual(b4.block_problems(rec, rows), [])
+        rows, _ = self.run_frames([self.ok(1)] * 10 + [self.ok(n) for n in range(2, 500)])
+        self.assertEqual(sum(r['kind'] == 'camera_stop' for r in rows), 4)  # the 4th ends the phase
+        self.assertTrue(any('"no new"' in w for w in b4.block_problems(record('Y', rows)[0], rows)))
+
+    def test_camera_overrun_and_last_move_cut(self):
+        rows, over = self.run_frames([self.ok(n) for n in range(1, 500)], detect_s=0.5)
+        self.assertEqual(over, 0.0)  # the move after the last cycle is cut at the deadline
+        self.assertTrue(all(r['t'] < 180 for r in rows))
+        self.assertEqual(round(rows[1]['t'] - rows[0]['t'], 3), 2.0)
+        rows, over = self.run_frames([self.ok(n) for n in range(1, 500)], detect_s=40.0)
+        self.assertGreater(over, 0)
+        self.assertLessEqual(over, 40.0)
+

+class Ownership(unittest.TestCase):
+    """A camera start that begins capture and then raises: stop_camera is still called; every cleanup runs."""
+
+    def setUp(self):
+        self.saved = (b4.make_robot, b4.reading, b4.cores_ok, b4.repro.agents, b4.read_frame, b4.watch_check,
+                      b4.start_server, b4.stop_server, b4.picker_setup, b4.warm, b4.stop_camera)
+        b4.reading = lambda pids=None, **kw: good()
+        b4.cores_ok = lambda: True
+        b4.repro.agents = lambda: ''
+        b4.read_frame = lambda **kw: {'status': 'missing'}
+        b4.watch_check = lambda targets: {'mono': 0, 'hits': []}
+
+    def tearDown(self):
+        (b4.make_robot, b4.reading, b4.cores_ok, b4.repro.agents, b4.read_frame, b4.watch_check,
+         b4.start_server, b4.stop_server, b4.picker_setup, b4.warm, b4.stop_camera) = self.saved
+
+    def ctx(self, smoke=False):
+        return {'session': 1, 'smoke': smoke, 'dur': 0.05, 'test': {}, 'frames': {}}
+
+    def test_failed_start_is_stopped(self):
+        robot = FakeRobot([], fail_start=True)
+        b4.make_robot = lambda: robot
+        rec, rows = b4.run_block('Y', Path('.'), self.ctx(), lambda r: None)
+        self.assertEqual(robot.stops, 1)
+        self.assertFalse(robot.capturing)
+        self.assertEqual(rec['label'], 'INVALID')
+
+    def test_each_cleanup_runs_when_another_fails(self):
+        robot = FakeRobot([], fail_start=True)
+        b4.make_robot = lambda: robot
+        stopped = []
+
+        class Proc:
+            pid = 4242
+        b4.start_server = lambda out, name, cmd: (Proc(), None, 0.1)
+        b4.stop_server = lambda proc, log: stopped.append(proc) or (0, False)
+        b4.picker_setup = lambda test: ({'items': [{'id': 1}]}, {('V3', 'FWD', 1): None}, [])
+        b4.warm = lambda prefix: {}
+        b4.stop_camera = lambda r: (_ for _ in ()).throw(OSError('broadcast failed'))
+        with unittest.mock.patch.object(b4.Periodic, 'stop', side_effect=RuntimeError('join')), \
+                unittest.mock.patch.object(b4, 'decide', return_value={'id': 1, 'wall_s': 0.0}), \
+                unittest.mock.patch.object(b4, 'slot', return_value={'action': 'restore', 'wall_s': 0.0}):
+            rec, rows = b4.run_block('PY', Path('.'), {**self.ctx(), 'dur': 0.01}, lambda r: None)
+        self.assertEqual(len(stopped), 1)  # the server is stopped although watch and camera cleanup failed
+        self.assertTrue(any('cleanup camera' in e for e in rec['errors']))
+        self.assertTrue(any('cleanup watch' in e for e in rec['errors']))
+        self.assertEqual(rec['label'], 'INVALID')
+
+    def test_initial_checks_abort_before_any_setup(self):
+        b4.repro.agents = lambda: '42 claude'
+        b4.start_server = lambda *a: self.fail('server started')
+        b4.make_robot = lambda: self.fail('camera started')
+        rec, rows = b4.run_block('PY', Path('.'), self.ctx(), lambda r: None)
+        self.assertEqual(rec['label'], 'INVALID')
+        self.assertIn('no workload started', ' '.join(rec['errors']))
+

+class Definition(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_changes_are_refused(self):
+        d = {'files': {'main.py': 'a', 'benchmark/x.py': 'b'}, 'commands': {'P': 'x'}, 'frames': {'1': 'f'}}
+        self.assertEqual(b4.definition_diff(d, json.loads(json.dumps(d))), [])
+        self.assertEqual(b4.definition_diff(d, {**d, 'files': {**d['files'], 'main.py': 'z'}}),
+                         [('files', 'main.py', 'a', 'z')])
+        self.assertTrue(b4.definition_diff(d, {**d, 'frames': {'1': 'g'}}))
+        self.assertTrue(b4.definition_diff(d, {**d, 'files': {**d['files'], 'server_manager.py': 'c'}}))  # new dep
+        self.assertTrue(b4.definition_diff(d, {**d, 'commands': {'P': 'x --threads 8'}}))
+
+    def test_real_definition(self):
+        fr = b4.frames()
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as t:
+            d = b4.definition(Path(t), fr)
+        files = d['files']
+        for f in ('benchmark/mind_picker/b4_baseline/b4.py', 'benchmark/mind_picker/b4_baseline/oneshot.sh',
+                  'main.py', 'server_manager.py', 'detect_person.py', 'benchmark/mind_picker/bc_build/bc.py'):
+            self.assertIn(f, files)
+        self.assertEqual(sorted(d['frames']), sorted(str(i) for i in b4.FRAME_IDS))
+        self.assertEqual(set(d['commands']), set(b4.SERVER_BLOCKS))
+
+    def test_resume_refuses_a_changed_frame(self):
+        fr = b4.frames()
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as t:
+            before = b4.definition(Path(t), fr)
+            frame = b4.bv.FRAMES_DIR / Path(fr[1][0]['frame_file']).name
+            with unittest.mock.patch.object(b4, 'sha', side_effect=lambda b: 'changed' if b == frame.read_bytes()
+                                            else b4.bc.sha(b)):
+                after = b4.definition(Path(t), fr)
+        self.assertEqual([x[:2] for x in b4.definition_diff(before, after)], [('frames', '1')])
+

   1  #!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/python
   2  """MIND-B2: picker quality with the policy written as explicit rules (V1), rules + worked examples (V2), rules +
   3  examples + one reason sentence first (V3). Research only: no motors, camera, YOLO, M2, main.py or run_mission.py; no
   4  MTP, no projector. Accuracy is deterministic (temperature 0); timings are recorded but are NOT MEASUREMENTS.
   5  
   6  Actions: explore, approach (B1's visit renamed), greet, look, wait. Only allowed actions are offered and scored,
   7  renormalised over them; ties: first offered.
   8  DEV = b1_quality/set/b1_set.json (SHA-256 pinned, read 'visit' as 'approach'). TEST = 40 items kept OUTSIDE the repo
   9  (WORKFLOW test-set custody) at ~/mind_b2_test/b2_test.json, SHA-256 pinned below (recorded before any Gemma call);
  10  load_test() refuses to open it until selection.json exists and its logged SHA-256 matches; a TEST path inside the repo
  11  (also through a symlink) is refused before it is read.
  12  Rendering: Gemma 4 template (b0.render, checked against /apply-template, thinking off); system = SYS_RULES (V1) or
  13  SYS_RULES + 'Examples:' (V2, V3); user = B1 arm B state lines (gerund activity, Allowed actions in catalogue order), a
  14  blank line, the tail with the allowed names (FWD catalogue order, REV reversed); cache_prompt true.
  15    V1, V2: first-token scoring over the names right after the model turn.
  16    V3: step 1 generates the reason (n_predict 48, stop at the first newline); step 2 appends '\\nAction:' to the model
  17        turn and scores the next token over ' <name>' (the names after a space, as 'Action: wait' tokenizes).
  18  Names must have distinct first tokens in the scored position: checked with /tokenize for every DEV prompt before any
  19  inference (V3 with an empty reason), again for each V3 item with its actual reason, and for the TEST prompts before
  20  TEST inference; a problem STOPs (INVALID).
  21  One server launch (mem1.command(2048) = setup_q4 flags, pins checked); DEV blocks V1-FWD .. V3-REV; select() (fixed
  22  rule) -> selection.json, SHA-256 logged; TEST: the selected variant FWD then REV. PAUSE_S between blocks. Metrics per
  23  block are B1's (b1.metrics); PASS on TEST is B1's rule (acceptable >= 0.80 and strict >= 0.65 in both orders, flips
  24  <= 4). VALID only with all 320 rows without error, one server PID, exit 0 without SIGKILL, no exception or stop, and
  25  both set hashes unchanged at the end.

  27  Usage: b2.py [--test PATH]   (Termux python; from proot Debian or native Termux)

BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b2_rules/b2.py (488 lines, excerpt lines 147-150)
 147  def state(s, it):
 148      return '\n'.join([f'Mission: {s["missions"][it["mission"]]}', f'Current activity: {ACTIVITY[it["current"]]}',
 149                        f'What just happened: {it["event"]}', f'Memory: {it["memory"] or "none"}',
 150                        f'Allowed actions: {", ".join(it["allowed"])}'])
END

BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b2_rules/b2.py (488 lines, excerpt lines 157-162)
 157  def tail(variant, names):
 158      words = f'{", ".join(names[:-1])} or {names[-1]}.'
 159      if variant == 'V3':
 160          return ('Which action next? First write one short sentence naming the rule that fits. Then write a new line '
 161                  f"starting with 'Action:' and one word: {words}")
 162      return f'Which action next? Answer with one word: {words}'
END
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b2_rules/b2.py (488 lines, excerpt lines 165-167)
 165  def messages(s, it, variant, order):
 166      return [{'role': 'system', 'content': SYSTEM[variant]},
 167              {'role': 'user', 'content': state(s, it) + '\n\n' + tail(variant, offered(it, order))}]
END
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b2_rules/b2.py (488 lines, excerpt lines 170-181)
 170  def first_ids(post, text, ids, names, sep=''):
 171      """({name: first token id after text}, problems): text's ids must stay a prefix; first tokens distinct."""
 172      out, why = {}, []
 173      for n in names:
 174          full = b0.tokenize(post, text + sep + n)
 175          if full[:len(ids)] != ids or len(full) == len(ids):
 176              why.append(f'{n!r}: prompt ids change or no token follows')
 177          else:
 178              out[n] = full[len(ids)]
 179      if len(set(out.values())) != len(out):
 180          why.append(f'first tokens not distinct: {out}')
 181      return out, why
END
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b2_rules/b2.py (488 lines, excerpt lines 184-208)
 184  def preflight(post, s, blocks, info, tag):
 185      """prompts[(variant, order, id)] = (text, ids, {name: id}); every prompt rendered and tokenized, no /completion.
 186      Problems go to info['problems']."""
 187      prompts, sizes = {}, {}
 188      for v, order in blocks:
 189          for it in s['items']:
 190              names = offered(it, order)
 191              text, ids, bad = b0.render(post, messages(s, it, v, order))
 192              if v == 'V3':  # scored position checked with an empty reason; rechecked per item with the real one
 193                  t2 = text + ACTION
 194                  nid, why = first_ids(post, t2, b0.tokenize(post, t2), names, ' ')
 195              else:
 196                  nid, why = first_ids(post, text, ids, names)
 197              info['problems'] += [f'{tag} {v}-{order} item {it["id"]}: {w}' for w in why + ([bad] if bad else [])]
 198              prompts[v, order, it['id']] = (text, ids, nid)
 199              sizes.setdefault(v, []).append(len(ids))
 200              info.setdefault('name_ids', {}).setdefault('V3' if v == 'V3' else 'V1/V2', {}).update(nid)
 201              if tag == 'DEV' and it['id'] == SHOW_ITEM and order == 'FWD':
 202                  info[f'prompt_{v}_dev_item{SHOW_ITEM}'] = post('/detokenize', {'tokens': ids})['content']
 203      for v in sizes:
 204          info.setdefault('system_tokens', {})[v] = len(b0.tokenize(post, SYSTEM[v], special=False))
 205          info.setdefault('prompt_tokens', {})[f'{tag} {v}'] = (min(sizes[v]), max(sizes[v]))
 206          if max(sizes[v]) + CTX_MARGIN > CTX:
 207              info['problems'].append(f'{tag} {v}: longest prompt {max(sizes[v])} + {CTX_MARGIN} > {CTX}')
 208      return prompts
END
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b2_rules/b2.py (488 lines, excerpt lines 211-238)
 211  def score(post, it, v, order, prompt):
 212      """One row: probabilities over the offered names (raw and renormalised), top, margin, counters; V3 adds the
 213      reason and its step-1 counters."""
 214      names, (text, ids, nid) = offered(it, order), prompt
 215      t0, row = time.perf_counter(), {'variant': v, 'order': order, 'id': it['id'], 'offered': names}
 216      if v == 'V3':
 217          r1 = post('/completion', {'prompt': ids, **b0.DECODE, **REASON, 'cache_prompt': True})
 218          reason = r1['content']
 219          t2 = text + reason + ACTION
 220          ids = b0.tokenize(post, t2)
 221          nid, why = first_ids(post, t2, ids, names, ' ')
 222          if why:
 223              raise RuntimeError(f'scored position after the reason: {why}')
 224          tm1 = r1.get('timings', {})
 225          row.update(reason=reason, reason_tokens=r1.get('tokens_predicted'), reason_stop=r1.get('stop_type'),
 226                     reason_prompt_n=tm1.get('prompt_n'), reason_cache_n=tm1.get('cache_n'))
 227      body = {'prompt': ids, **b0.DECODE, **b0.PICK, 'cache_prompt': True}
 228      res = post('/completion', body)
 229      raw, norm = b0.probs(res, {n: [nid[n]] for n in names})
 230      if norm is None:
 231          raise RuntimeError(f'item {it["id"]}: an offered name is outside the top {b0.PICK["n_probs"]}: {raw}')
 232      top = max(norm, key=norm.get)  # ties: first offered
 233      second = sorted(norm.values())[-2] if len(norm) > 1 else 0.0
 234      tm = res.get('timings', {})
 235      row.update(prompt_tokens=len(ids), prompt_ids_sha256=sha(json.dumps(ids)), prompt_n=tm.get('prompt_n'),
 236                 cache_n=tm.get('cache_n'), prompt_ms=tm.get('prompt_ms'), wall_s=round(time.perf_counter() - t0, 3),
 237                 probs_raw=raw, probs=norm, top=top, margin=norm[top] - second)
 238      return row
END
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b0b_slots/b0b.py (345 lines, excerpt lines 160-163)
 160  def slot_action(post, action, info):
 161      t0 = time.perf_counter()

BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b0_cache/b0.py (344 lines, excerpt lines 109-117)
 109  def probs(res, ids_by_name):
 110      """Raw probabilities of the names' tokens at the first generated position (picker.Gemma.decide), None outside
 111      the top n_probs; renormalised over the five only when all are present."""
 112      by_id = {e['id']: (e['prob'] if 'prob' in e else math.exp(e['logprob']))
 113               for e in res['completion_probabilities'][0]['top_logprobs']}
 114      raw = {n: by_id.get(i[0]) for n, i in ids_by_name.items()}
 115      mass = sum(v for v in raw.values() if v is not None)
 116      norm = {n: v / mass for n, v in raw.items()} if None not in raw.values() and mass > 0 else None
 117      return raw, norm
END

 283  def lcp(a, b):
 284      n = 0
 285      while n < min(len(a), len(b)) and a[n] == b[n]:
 286          n += 1
 287      return n

BEGIN benchmark/memory_context/mem1.py (2120 lines, excerpt lines 510-518)
 510  def sections(out):
 511      """{name: (rc, text)} of root_script's '=== name' sections; rc from the closing 'rc=N' line, None when the
 512      section did not finish."""
 513      parts = re.split(r'^=== (\w+)\n', out, flags=re.M)
 514      got = {}
 515      for name, body in zip(parts[1::2], parts[2::2]):
 516          m = re.search(r'^rc=(\d+)\s*\Z', body, re.M)
 517          got[name] = (int(m.group(1)), body[:m.start()]) if m else (None, body)
 518      return got
END
BEGIN benchmark/memory_context/mem1.py (2120 lines, excerpt lines 595-609)
 595  def pin_sampler():
 596      """Ask for the calling (sampler) thread on SAMPLER_CPUS at nice SAMPLER_NICE (both per thread on Linux; its su
 597      clients inherit them) and record what it actually got: a refused or ignored request is recorded, never assumed."""
 598      tid = threading.get_native_id()
 599      st = {'tid': tid, 'requested_cpus': list(SAMPLER_CPUS), 'requested_nice': SAMPLER_NICE}
 600      try:
 601          os.sched_setaffinity(0, SAMPLER_CPUS)
 602      except OSError as e:
 603          st['affinity_error'] = str(e)
 604      try:
 605          os.setpriority(os.PRIO_PROCESS, tid, SAMPLER_NICE)
 606      except OSError as e:
 607          st['nice_error'] = str(e)
 608      st.update(cpus=sorted(os.sched_getaffinity(0)), nice=os.getpriority(os.PRIO_PROCESS, tid))
 609      return st
END

1270      """Nearest-rank 95th percentile."""

BEGIN benchmark/power_map/power_map.py (654 lines, excerpt lines 53-65)
  53  def thermal_gate(path, idle, label, smoke):
  54      """Same skin gate, without the unrelated CPU-core term; bounded to eight minutes."""
  55      began = time.monotonic()
  56      while True:
  57          thermal, dump = cr.read_thermal(path), cr.read_dump()
  58          cool = dump['skin'] is not None and dump['skin'] <= idle['skin'] + cr.SKIN_GATE_C
  59          waited = time.monotonic() - began
  60          if smoke or cool or waited >= GATE_MAX_S:
  61              return {**thermal, 'skin': dump['skin'], 'status': dump['status'],
  62                      'waited_s': round(waited), 'warm_start': not (smoke or cool)}
  63          print(f'[{label}] waiting: skin {dump["skin"]}, need <= {idle["skin"] + cr.SKIN_GATE_C:.1f}; '
  64                f'{waited:.0f} s', flush=True)
  65          time.sleep(cr.GATE_POLL_S)
END

BEGIN benchmark/coresidency/coresidency.py (1290 lines, excerpt lines 125-133)
 125  def root(cmd, tag, timeout=60):
 126      """DECISIONS #123 form: su -c "<cmd> </dev/null >FILE 2>&1", then the file is read. Returns (rc, text).
 127      su's own stderr (e.g. a refused su) is kept for this thread: root_stderr()."""
 128      path = f'/data/local/tmp/coresidency_{tag}.txt'
 129      _root_local.stderr = ''
 130      r = subprocess.run(['su', '-c', f'{{ {cmd}; }} </dev/null >{path} 2>&1; rc=$?; cat {path}; exit $rc'],
 131                         stdin=DEVNULL, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout)
 132      _root_local.stderr = r.stderr or ''
 133      return r.returncode, r.stdout
END
BEGIN benchmark/coresidency/coresidency.py (1290 lines, excerpt lines 136-138)
 136  def root_stderr():
 137      """su's stderr of this thread's last root() call ('' if none or if that call raised)."""
 138      return getattr(_root_local, 'stderr', '')
END

BEGIN benchmark/coresidency/coresidency.py (1290 lines, excerpt lines 697-713)
 697  def parse_dump(text):
 698      """Android thermal status and the 'Current temperatures from HAL' section (name -> degC)."""
 699      status = re.search(r'^Thermal Status: (\d+)\s*$', text, re.M)
 700      sections = {}
 701      for m in re.finditer(r'^(\S[^\n]*):\n((?:[ \t]+[^\n]*\n?)*)', text, re.M):
 702          sections[m.group(1).strip()] = m.group(2)
 703      hal = {}
 704      for m in TEMP_RE.finditer(sections.get('Current temperatures from HAL', '')):
 705          try:
 706              v = float(m.group(1))
 707          except ValueError:
 708              continue
 709          if not math.isnan(v):
 710              hal[m.group(3)] = v
 711      cached = sorted({m.group(3) for m in TEMP_RE.finditer(sections.get('Cached temperatures', ''))})
 712      return {'status': int(status.group(1)) if status else None, 'skin': hal.get(SKIN), 'hal': hal,
 713              'cached_names': cached}
END
BEGIN benchmark/llm_objective_setting/conversation/chat_mtp/chat_mtp.py (478 lines, excerpt lines 106-125)
 106  def refusals():
 107      """Reasons not to start (cr.require_native runs before this)."""
 108      why = []
 109      agents = subprocess.run(['pgrep', '-fa', AGENTS], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
 110      if agents:
 111          why.append(f'agent process running: {agents}')
 112      with socket.socket() as s:
 113          s.settimeout(2)
 114          if s.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', 8080)) == 0:
 115              why.append('port 8080 in use')
 116      rc, out = cr.root('cat /sys/class/power_supply/battery/capacity /sys/class/power_supply/battery/status', 'battery')
 117      try:
 118          level, status = out.split()[:2]
 119          if int(level) < MIN_BATTERY:
 120              why.append(f'battery {level} % < {MIN_BATTERY} %')
 121          if status != 'Discharging':
 122              why.append(f'battery status {status}, not Discharging: unplug the charger')
 123      except ValueError:
 124          why.append(f'battery unreadable (su rc {rc}: {out[:100]!r})')
 125      return why
END

BEGIN main.py (690 lines, excerpt lines 222-254)
 222      def start_camera(self, heat=None):
 223          """heat: the mission's HeatControl, for the detector trace lines (None: no trace)."""
 224          created = self.detector is None
 225          # Shared by detect_scene and the robot loop. MID cores (#128); raises before `am start` when the
 226          # ORT workers or an affinity cannot be verified, so the mission does not start.
 227          self.detector = get_detector(pinned=True)
 228          if created and heat:
 229              heat.event('detector_config', {**self.detector.config(), 'camera_rate': CAMERA_RATE})
 230          # Affinity is a power setting, not a safety stop: a failed re-pin is logged and the mission continues.
 231          affinity = self.detector.check_affinity()
 232          if not affinity['ok']:
 233              print(f"[DETECTOR] affinity re-pin failed: {affinity['errors']} — continuing")
 234          if heat:
 235              heat.event('detector_affinity', affinity)
 236          self.slot = new_slot()
 237          self.slot_n += 1
 238          self.last_640 = None
 239          if self.camera_source == 'robotcam':
 240              self.camera_session = None
 241              self.camera_frame = None
 242              started_boot_s = time.clock_gettime(time.CLOCK_BOOTTIME)
 243              subprocess.run(['am', 'start', '-n', 'com.pixelrobot.robotcam/.StartActivity',
 244                              '--es', 'mode', 'B', '--ei', 'rate', str(CAMERA_RATE)],
 245                             check=True, timeout=10, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
 246                             stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
 247              deadline = time.monotonic() + 8
 248              while time.monotonic() < deadline:
 249                  result = read_frame(min_capture_boot_s=started_boot_s)
 250                  if result['status'] == 'ok':
 251                      self.camera_session = result['session']
 252                      return
 253                  time.sleep(0.1)
 254              raise RuntimeError('RobotCam did not publish a usable frame')
END

 259                              '-a', 'com.pixelrobot.robotcam.STOP'],
 260                             check=True, timeout=5, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
 261                             stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
