BEGIN main.py (690 lines, excerpt lines 263-280)
 263      def move(self, cmd, duration=CYCLE_MOVE_TIME, speed=MOTOR_SPEED):
 264          print(f'  [MOTOR] {cmd}')
 265          if self.motors:
 266              if cmd == 'FORWARD':  self.motors.forward(speed, duration)
 267              elif cmd == 'LEFT':   self.motors.rotate_left(120, duration)
 268              elif cmd == 'RIGHT':  self.motors.rotate_right(120, duration)
 269              elif cmd == 'BACK':   self.motors.backward(MOTOR_SPEED, duration)
 270              elif cmd == 'STOP':   self.motors.stop()
 271              elif cmd == 'HOLD':   # stand still for the cycle; the mission continues (GREET, person-stop)
 272                  self.motors.stop()
 273                  time.sleep(duration)
 274              elif cmd == 'STRAFE_RIGHT': self.motors.strafe_right(100, duration)
 275              elif cmd == 'STRAFE_LEFT':  self.motors.strafe_left(100, duration)
 276          else:
 277              time.sleep(duration)
 278          self.last_moves.append(cmd)
 279          if len(self.last_moves) > 10:
 280              self.last_moves.pop(0)
END
BEGIN main.py (690 lines, excerpt lines 383-462)
 383      def run_cycle(self, photo_path=None):
 384          self.cycle += 1
 385          self.slot['cycles'] += 1
 386          self.collect_pick()  # a pick that finished meanwhile changes the mode from this cycle on
 387          age = None
 388          distance = self.get_distance()
 389          print(f'\n── Cycle {self.cycle} | {self.heat_text} | dist:{distance}cm ──')
 390  
 391          # Vision: every cycle must have a fresh usable frame before any movement.
 392          if photo_path:
 393              path = photo_path
 394          elif self.camera_source == 'robotcam':
 395              frame_result = read_frame(session=self.camera_session)
 396              if frame_result['status'] != 'ok' or (self.camera_frame is not None and
 397                      frame_result['frame'] <= self.camera_frame):
 398                  reason = frame_result['status'] if frame_result['status'] != 'ok' else 'no new'
 399                  print(f'  [CAM] {reason} frame — stop')
 400                  self.slot['camera_stops'][reason] = self.slot['camera_stops'].get(reason, 0) + 1
 401                  if self.motors: self.motors.stop()
 402                  return 'STOP'
 403              self.camera_session = frame_result['session']
 404              self.camera_frame = frame_result['frame']
 405              frame = frame_result['image']
 406              age = frame_result['age_s']
 407              self.slot['frames'] += 1
 408              self.slot['age_s'].append(age)
 409          else:
 410              if not take_photo(PHOTO_A):
 411                  print('  [CAM] Photo failed')
 412                  if self.motors: self.motors.stop()
 413                  return 'STOP'
 414              path = PHOTO_A
 415  
 416          detections = []
 417          try:
 418              if photo_path:
 419                  results = detect_scene(path)
 420              else:
 421                  if self.detector is None: self.detector = get_detector()
 422                  size = self.size_policy.next_size()
 423                  if self.camera_source == 'termux_photo':
 424                      from PIL import Image, ImageOps
 425                      with Image.open(path) as source:
 426                          frame = ImageOps.exif_transpose(source).convert('RGB')
 427                  started = time.perf_counter()
 428                  detections = self.detector.detect(frame, size)
 429                  detect_ms = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000
 430                  self.slot['detect_ms'][size].append(detect_ms)
 431                  self.size_policy.observe(detections, frame.height)
 432                  results = scene_from_detections(detections, *frame.size)
 433                  if size == 640:
 434                      self.last_640 = (frame, detections)
 435                  print(f'  [YOLO] size={size} person={any(d["class_name"] == "person" for d in detections)} '
 436                        f'detect={detect_ms:.0f}ms age={"n/a" if age is None else f"{age:.2f}s"}')
 437          except Exception as exc:
 438              print(f'  [VISION] {exc} — stop')
 439              if self.motors: self.motors.stop()
 440              return 'STOP'
 441          scene = scene_to_text(results)
 442          self.scene_log.append(scene)
 443          if len(self.scene_log) > 10:
 444              self.scene_log.pop(0)
 445          print(f'  [YOLO] {scene}')
 446  
 447          # Fast navigation rules, shaped by the mode; person-stop last. The legacy tuples keep one person (the most
 448          # confident); person-stop also sees every other person detection's area bucket.
 449          people = [r[3] for r in results if r[0] == 'person'] + [
 450              scene_from_detections([d], *frame.size)[0][3] for d in detections if d['class_name'] == 'person']
 451          move = self.choose_move(results, distance, people)
 452          print(f'  [NAV] {move}')
 453          self.request_pick(results, distance)  # never waits; M2 runs during the move below
 454  
 455          # Execute move (motors cool during movement)
 456          if move != 'STOP':
 457              self.move(move)
 458          else:
 459              print('  [STOP] Mission complete or waiting')
 460              self.state = 'found'
 461  
 462          return move
END
BEGIN detect_person.py (283 lines, excerpt lines 136-146)
 136      def affinity(self):
 137          """{tid: sorted cpus or error text} for the caller and workers; read only."""
 138          live, out = thread_ids(), {}
 139          for tid in [self.caller_tid] + self.worker_tids:
 140              try:
 141                  if tid not in live:  # never touch a tid that left this process (it could be reused elsewhere)
 142                      raise ProcessLookupError(f'thread {tid} is gone')
 143                  out[tid] = sorted(os.sched_getaffinity(tid))
 144              except OSError as e:
 145                  out[tid] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 146          return out
END
BEGIN detect_person.py (283 lines, excerpt lines 148-160)
 148      def check_affinity(self):
 149          """Re-pin and verify any of the 3 threads whose affinity drifted (Android cpuset changes can reset it).
 150          Never raises: the result says ok or not."""
 151          before = self.affinity()
 152          repin = [tid for tid, cpus in before.items() if isinstance(cpus, list) and set(cpus) != self.cpus]
 153          errors = {tid: cpus for tid, cpus in before.items() if isinstance(cpus, str)}
 154          for tid in repin:
 155              try:
 156                  pin(tid, self.cpus)
 157              except (OSError, RuntimeError) as e:
 158                  errors[tid] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 159          after = self.affinity() if repin else before
 160          return {'before': before, 'repinned': repin, 'after': after, 'ok': not errors, 'errors': errors}
END
BEGIN detect_person.py (283 lines, excerpt lines 162-168)
 162      def config(self):
 163          return {'ort_version': ort.__version__,
 164                  'sessions': {size: {'intra_op_num_threads': s.get_session_options().intra_op_num_threads,
 165                                      'inter_op_num_threads': s.get_session_options().inter_op_num_threads}
 166                               for size, s in self.detector.sessions.items()},
 167                  'worker_tids': self.worker_tids, 'caller_tid': self.caller_tid,
 168                  'requested_cpus': sorted(self.cpus), 'affinity': self.affinity()}

BEGIN detect_person.py (283 lines, excerpt lines 173-181)
 173  def get_detector(pinned=False):
 174      """Shared detector. pinned=True (the robot process) creates the PinnedDetector; later get_detector() calls,
 175      e.g. from detect_scene(), then return that same one. Without pinned it is a plain Detector(), as before."""
 176      global _detector
 177      if _detector is None:
 178          _detector = PinnedDetector() if pinned else Detector()
 179      elif pinned and not isinstance(_detector, PinnedDetector):
 180          raise RuntimeError('a default detector is already loaded; the robot needs one pinned session pair')
 181      return _detector
END
BEGIN run_mission.py (118 lines, excerpt lines 31-114)
  31  def main(argv=None):
  32      argv = sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv
  33      dry = '--dry' in argv
  34      camera_source = 'termux_photo' if '--termux-photo' in argv else 'robotcam'
  35      person_search = '--person-search' in argv
  36      use_picker = '--no-picker' not in argv
  37      args = [a for a in argv if not a.startswith('--')]
  38      mission = args[0] if args else 'explore and map the rooms'
  39  
  40      signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, exit_on_signal)
  41      signal.signal(signal.SIGHUP, exit_on_signal)
  42      motors = None
  43      robot = None
  44      heat = None
  45      pick = None
  46      try:
  47          # Before the motor reader and heat threads exist, so the ORT thread diff sees only ORT workers;
  48          # raises (mission not started) when the workers or their affinity cannot be verified.
  49          R.get_detector(pinned=True)
  50          # Also before those threads: Relate's ORT thread diff must see only its own worker.
  51          pick, picker_off = build_picker() if use_picker else (None, '--no-picker')
  52          if use_picker and pick is None:
  53              print(f'[PICKER] disabled: {picker_off} — the mission continues in EXPLORE')
  54          if not dry:
  55              from motors import Motors
  56              motors = Motors()
  57              motors.connect()
  58              time.sleep(1)
  59              print('PING:', 'ALIVE' if motors.ping() else 'NO REPLY')
  60              print('distance:', motors.get_distance(), 'cm')
  61          robot = R.Robot(motors, camera_source=camera_source)
  62          if person_search:
  63              robot.size_policy.set_mode('person_search')
  64          if use_picker:
  65              robot.picker = pick
  66              robot.set_mode('EXPLORE', 'mission start')
  67          heat = heat_control.HeatControl(heat_control.HeatMonitor())
  68          heat.start()  # raises without root or a first complete reading: mission not started
  69          heat.event('picker_config', {'enabled': True, **pick.config()} if pick else {'enabled': False, 'reason': picker_off})
  70          robot.start_camera(heat)
  71          if not dry:
  72              R.warm_up()
  73          print(f"\nmission: {mission}{'   [DRY — no motor commands]' if dry else ''}")
  74          print('ctrl-C to stop\n')
  75          robot.run_mission(mission, heat=heat)
  76      except KeyboardInterrupt:
  77          print('\ninterrupted')
  78      finally:
  79          for signum in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGHUP):
  80              signal.signal(signum, signal.SIG_IGN)
  81          cleanup_errors = []
  82          if motors:
  83              try:
  84                  motors.stop()
  85              except Exception as exc:
  86                  cleanup_errors.append(f'[MOTOR] stop failed: {exc}')
  87          if robot:
  88              try:
  89                  robot.stop_camera()
  90              except Exception as exc:
  91                  cleanup_errors.append(f'[CAM] stop failed: {exc}')
  92          if pick:
  93              if not pick.stop():
  94                  cleanup_errors.append('[PICKER] worker still running after its timeout')
  95              if robot:
  96                  try:
  97                      robot.collect_pick()  # trace a pick that ended during shutdown
  98                  except Exception as exc:
  99                      cleanup_errors.append(f'[PICKER] last pick not traced: {exc}')
 100          if heat:
 101              try:
 102                  heat.stop()
 103              except Exception as exc:
 104                  cleanup_errors.append(f'[HEAT] stop failed: {exc}')
 105          if motors:
 106              try:
 107                  motors.disconnect()
 108              except Exception as exc:
 109                  cleanup_errors.append(f'[MOTOR] disconnect failed: {exc}')
 110          for error in cleanup_errors:
 111              print(error)
 112          if robot:
 113              print(f'rooms: {robot.known_rooms}')
 114              print(f'moves: {robot.last_moves}')
END
